				culture_group = japanese_g				
		}
			}

				culture_group = japanese_g				
		}
			}	
	picture = COURT_eventPicture
	}

				culture_group = japanese_g				
			}

	goto = dignitarycapital

	is_triggered_only = yes

		}

# train spy from
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_psort_events.50
	title = cosmo_psort_events.50.t
	desc = cosmo_psort_events.50.d
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {
				culture_group = japanese_g				
		}
			}	
	picture = COURT_eventPicture
	}	
	picture = {
	trigger = {
				culture_group = japanese_g				
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_OOKU_GENERAL_8_eventPicture
	}
	
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		}


	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
				random_known_country = {
				limit = {
					reverse_has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = cosmo_spy_sent
					}
				}
					save_event_target_as = spycountry
				}
		}
		}		
		
	
	option = { #artist
		name = cosmo_psort_events.50.a	
		event_target:spycountry = {
		country_event = {#additional option menus
			id = cosmo_psort_events.5
			}	
		}																																																	
	}						
}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no		
		}


	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
				random_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_province_flag = send_entertain_orders
					}
				}
					save_event_target_as = prostituteprovince
					remove_building = entertain 
				}
		}
		}		
		
	
	option = {#sailors sort
		name = cosmo_psort_events.10.a
		trigger = {
			event_target:prostituteprovince = {
				NOT = {
					has_province_modifier = entertain_sailor_province
				}				
			cosmo_has_prostitute_trigger = no
			has_port = yes 	
				OR = {
					light_ships_in_province = 1
					heavy_ships_in_province = 1
					transports_in_province = 1
			has_building = shipyard
			has_building = dock
			has_building = grand_shipyard
			has_building = drydock
				}			
			}				
			has_estate_privilege = estate_madams_entertain_troops				
		}
			event_target:prostituteprovince = {
				custom_tooltip = send_entertain_sailors_tt 
				set_province_flag = entertain_here_now	
				set_province_flag = entertain_sailors_here
			}
			event_target:prostituteprovince = {
				add_scaled_local_dip_power = 3 
			}					
			set_country_flag = entertain_sailors_prostitutes
				country_event = {
			id = cosmo_brothel_events.501
			}
			every_province = {
				limit = {
					has_province_flag = send_entertain_orders
				}
				clr_province_flag = send_entertain_orders
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						estate_influence = {
							estate = estate_madams
							influence = 50
						}	
					}
				}
				add_years_of_income = -0.05
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					estate_influence = {
						estate = estate_madams
						influence = 50
					}
					NOT = {
						estate_influence = {
							estate = estate_madams
							influence = 75
						}	
					}
				}
				add_years_of_income = -0.1
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					estate_influence = {
						estate = estate_madams
						influence = 75
					}
					NOT = {
						estate_influence = {
							estate = estate_madams
							influence = 100
						}	
					}
				}
				add_years_of_income = -0.15
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					estate_influence = {
						estate = estate_madams
						influence = 100
					}
				}
				add_years_of_income = -0.2
			}					

		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
				which = cosmoPleasureCounter
				value = 20
				}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoPleasureCounter	
			value = 140
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoPleasureCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}				
		}								
	}

			which = cosmoPleasureCounter	
			value = 140
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoPleasureCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}				
		}							
	}		
	option = {#exit
		name = cosmo_psort_events.12.39
			every_province = {
				limit = {
					has_province_flag = cosmo_colonist_source
				}
				clr_province_flag = cosmo_colonist_source
			}
			every_province = {
				limit = {
					has_province_flag = colonist_here
				}
				clr_province_flag = colonist_here
			}				
	}
																
}

# entertain merchant repeater
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_psort_events.61
	title = cosmo_psort_events.61.t
	desc = cosmo_psort_events.61.d
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {
				culture_group = japanese_g				
		}
			}	
	picture = COURT_eventPicture
	}	
	picture = {
	trigger = {
				culture_group = japanese_g				
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_OOKU_GENERAL_8_eventPicture
	}
	
	is_triggered_only = yes

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		has_country_flag = cosmo_entertaining_merchants_repeating
		OR = {
		any_owned_province = {
			has_province_flag = cosmo_host_merchant_province
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_entertaining_merchants
			NOT = {
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_merchant_repeater
			}
		}
		any_subject_country = {
			any_owned_province = {
				has_province_flag = cosmo_host_merchant_province
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_entertaining_merchants
				NOT = {
					has_province_modifier = cosmo_merchant_repeater
				}
			}			
		}
		}
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {		
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_flag = cosmo_host_merchant_province
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_entertaining_merchants
				NOT = {
					has_province_modifier = cosmo_merchant_repeater
				}					
			}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_merchant_repeater
				duration = 90  
				hidden = yes
			}
			set_province_flag = entertain_merchants_here				
			save_event_target_as = merchantprovince
		}										
		}
		}		
		
	option = { #Advisors
		name = cosmo_psort_events.61.12	
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					has_opinion = {
						who = ROOT
						value = 1
					}					
					event_target:merchantprovince = {
						OR = {
							has_merchant = PREV 
							trade_share = {
								country = PREV
								share = 1
							}								
						}
					}
					NOT = { has_country_modifier = cosmo_merchant_target }
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					has_opinion = {
						who = ROOT
						value = 1
					}					
					event_target:merchantprovince = {
						OR = {
							has_merchant = PREV 
							trade_share = {
								country = PREV
								share = 1
							}								
						}
					}
					NOT = { has_country_modifier = cosmo_merchant_target }
					 
				}
			set_country_flag = entertain_merchant_country	
			country_event = {
				id = cosmo_usort_events.6010
			}			
			}			
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
				any_country = {
					has_opinion = {
						who = ROOT
						value = 1
					}					
					event_target:merchantprovince = {
						OR = {
							has_merchant = PREV 
							trade_share = {
								country = PREV
								share = 1
							}								
						}
					}
					NOT = { has_country_modifier = cosmo_merchant_target }
				}
				}
			}
			country_event = {
				id = cosmo_usort_events.60
			}			
		}		
	}	
						
}

#fakenw1
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_psort_events.6101
	title = cosmo_psort_events.6101.t
	desc = cosmo_psort_events.6101.d
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_FAKE_NEW_WORLD_MENU_1_eventPicture
	goto = prostituteprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
	}

	immediate = {
		hidden_effect = {
				random_province = {
					limit = {
						is_colony = yes 
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
						has_province_flag = colonist_here
					}
					save_event_target_as = colonyprovince
				}
				random_province = {
					limit = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT 
						has_province_flag = cosmo_colonist_source
					}
					save_event_target_as = prostituteprovince
				}			
		}
	}
	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_psort_events.6101.a		
		if = {
			limit = {
		NOT = {
					any_province = {
			has_province_modifier = fakenw_prostitute_1a
		}
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_fakenw_events.6201
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
					any_province = {
			has_province_modifier = fakenw_prostitute_1a
		}		
		}
			custom_tooltip = prostitute_gone_tt 		
			set_ruler_flag = cosmo_colony_carribeans_region_menus
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_psort_events.121
			}
		}						
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_fakenw_events.6101.b
		if = {
			limit = {
		NOT = {
					any_province = {
			has_province_modifier = fakenw_prostitute_1b
		}
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_fakenw_events.6202
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
					any_province = {
			has_province_modifier = fakenw_prostitute_1b
		}
		}
			custom_tooltip = prostitute_gone_tt 		
			set_ruler_flag = cosmo_colony_carribeans_region_menus
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_psort_events.121
			}
		}				
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_fakenw_events.6101.c
		if = {
			limit = {
		NOT = {
					any_province = {
			has_province_modifier = fakenw_prostitute_1c
		}
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_fakenw_events.6203
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
					any_province = {
			has_province_modifier = fakenw_prostitute_1c
		}
		}
			custom_tooltip = prostitute_gone_tt 		
			set_ruler_flag = cosmo_colony_carribeans_region_menus
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_psort_events.121
			}
		}				
		}		
		option = {#norefunds
		name = cosmo_fakenw_events.6101.7
		clr_ruler_flag = inspire_colonists_prostitutes
			every_province = {
				limit = {
					has_province_flag = cosmo_colonist_source
				}
				clr_province_flag = cosmo_colonist_source
			}
			every_province = {
				limit = {
					has_province_flag = colonist_here
				}
				clr_province_flag = colonist_here
			}			
		}			
}

			culture_group = penutian
			}
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_BROTHEL_DISTRICT_NORTH_AMERICA_1_eventPicture

	option = {#sailors sort
		name = cosmo_psort_events.11.a
		trigger = {
			event_target:prostituteprovince = {
				NOT = {
					has_province_modifier = entertain_sailor_province
				}				
			cosmo_has_prostitute_trigger = yes
			has_port = yes 	
				OR = {
					light_ships_in_province = 1
					heavy_ships_in_province = 1
					transports_in_province = 1
			has_building = shipyard
			has_building = dock
			has_building = grand_shipyard
			has_building = drydock
				}			
			}				
			has_estate_privilege = estate_madams_entertain_troops				
		}
			event_target:prostituteprovince = {
				add_scaled_local_dip_power = 3 
				custom_tooltip = send_entertain_sailors_tt 
				set_province_flag = entertain_sailors_here
				set_province_flag = entertain_sailors_present
				set_province_flag = cosmo_prostitute_options
			}		
			set_country_flag = entertain_sailors_prostitutes
			set_country_flag = entertain_sailors_prostitutes_current
				country_event = {
			id = cosmo_psort_events.8
			}
			every_province = {
				limit = {
					has_province_flag = send_entertain_orders
				}
				clr_province_flag = send_entertain_orders
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						estate_influence = {
							estate = estate_madams
							influence = 50
						}	
					}
				}
				add_years_of_income = -0.05
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					estate_influence = {
						estate = estate_madams
						influence = 50
					}
					NOT = {
						estate_influence = {
							estate = estate_madams
							influence = 75
						}	
					}
				}
				add_years_of_income = -0.1
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					estate_influence = {
						estate = estate_madams
						influence = 75
					}
					NOT = {
						estate_influence = {
							estate = estate_madams
							influence = 100
						}	
					}
				}
				add_years_of_income = -0.15
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					estate_influence = {
						estate = estate_madams
						influence = 100
					}
				}
				add_years_of_income = -0.2
			}					

		hidden_effect = {

				which = cosmoPleasureCounter
				value = 20
				}

		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoPleasureCounter

			}

# renew EST_VAL_ENTERTAIN_TROOPS
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_psort_events.81
	title = cosmo_psort_events.81.t
	desc = cosmo_psort_events.81.d
	picture = DIPLOMACY_eventPicture

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		has_estate_privilege = estate_madams_entertain_troops
		NOT = {
			has_estate_influence_modifier = {
				estate = estate_madams
				modifier = EST_VAL_ENTERTAIN_TROOPS
			}			
		}
		}

		mean_time_to_happen = {

			}			
		}
		}
